# Patron Characters
# For patrons characters, we ask them to have no more than 20 in a single attribute and no more than 60 in all attributes combined (prowess excepted in both cases). They are also limited to 5th generation. All of that post character creation (meaning after buffs like dynasty modifiers and such are applied).

# Khan of Han, Stephen Gawain	
# As an Anglo-Norman mercenary leader, Stephen Gawain joined the Varangian Guard and quickly rose through the ranks to become one of the empire's most important commanders in subsequent wars. His brutal and unprincipled tactics earned him comparisons to Roussel de Bailleul by the people of Constantinople. Although he tried to prove he would not betray them, the upper echelons of the empire, including possibly Emperor Alexios I himself, doubted his loyalty. Despite the distrust of the Byzantine Empire, Belisarius recognized his loyalty and suffering, and embraced him in early 1119. Throughout his life, Gowen remained loyal and used his cunning and brutal methods to secure his position as the Prince of Sinope/Popeiopolis, and covertly assisted the imperial army in repelling any invading enemies until the Bitter Crusaders destroyed the Byzantine Empire and the Dream of Kindred. During his career, he developed an underground romance with the empire's Grand Princess Anna Komnene. However, due to his loyalty, he did not become a part of the Grand Princess's conspiracy against her brother. After the Grand Princess's Embrace, he became a staunch supporter of her in the Dream. However, he quickly discovers that Anna is no longer the woman he once loved, but only a vicious, spiteful remnant. Now Stephen Gowan lives in pain, driven by the hope that his lover will once again become the person he love."	The archetype of the character is the protagonist of a Chinese novel called 鹰扬拜占庭. With the same name, Stephen Gawain in that novel somehow eloped with Anna and established their own kingdom in Anatolia and later take the throne of Constantinople. So I want to introduce this character into the World of Darkness.
